Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in boltzmann theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 926 and the invariant I(A,B) = 583 mod 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by boltzmann generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 926, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 583. QED.
